- **speckit.debt.log**: Record a technical debt as an entry under .specify/debt/&lt;slug&gt;/ — where it lives in the code, what it costs while unpaid, and what paying it looks like. Use when the user wants to log, register, note or track technical debt, a shortcut, a hack, a TODO or FIXME worth remembering, a 'we will fix it later', or anything deliberately left imperfect — and right after an implementation took a shortcut worth writing down. Reads code, never writes it. ([leolicona/guide-me](https://github.com/leolicona/guide-me/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/debt/commands/speckit.debt.log.md))
- **speckit.debt.pay**: Close a technical debt entry under .specify/debt/&lt;slug&gt;/ only after verifying against the code that its exit condition is met, recording the evidence in payment.md with a verdict of verified, partial, unpaid or not-run. Use when the user says a debt was paid, fixed, resolved, cleaned up or removed and wants the register updated, asks whether a debt is still there, or after a refactor or cleanup PR lands. Does not write the fix itself — that goes through the normal spec or lite-path flow. ([leolicona/guide-me](https://github.com/leolicona/guide-me/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/debt/commands/speckit.debt.pay.md))
- **speckit.debt.review**: Re-check every open technical debt entry under .specify/debt/ against the current codebase and write a snapshot to .specify/debt/review.md — which debts are still there, moved, already gone, or have spread. Use when the user asks what technical debt exists, how much there is, whether the debt register is still accurate or stale, what to pay first, or before planning a cleanup, refactor or maintenance sprint. Reads code only; changes no entry. ([leolicona/guide-me](https://github.com/leolicona/guide-me/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/debt/commands/speckit.debt.review.md))

- **speckit.house-process.graduation-review**: Run a feature's graduation: the house constitution's bar (design note, contract tests, eval suite, error handling and logging) unit by unit, the quality bars measured (Lighthouse, axe and the rest), an adversarial self-review with findings proven and triaged, the quickstart run, and a PR that reports it all. ([alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com](https://github.com/alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/house-process/commands/speckit.house-process.guided-review.md))

Use when a phase's PR is raised or merged, after context was compacted, or when the conversation has drifted across concerns. ([alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com](https://github.com/alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/house-process/commands/speckit.house-process.handoff.md))
- **speckit.house-process.harvest-lessons**: Review recent work for lessons that should be sent upstream to software-factory (or global norms) instead of staying trapped in this project ([alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com](https://github.com/alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/house-process/commands/speckit.house-process.harvest-lessons.md))
- **speckit.house-process.implement-brief**: Write the brief for a Spec Kit subagent (implement, or specify, plan, tasks): the slice's tasks, the skills it must load, the owner's answers word for word, privacy limits, what it may push, and what it reports back (token cost, skill gaps). Use before dispatching any /speckit-* command to a subagent. - **speckit.house-process.question-round**: Run a question round with the owner: draw out requirements and design decisions in focused rounds of up to four questions, each with a recommended option first, check stated facts against stored data, and record every answer with its reason in the decisions log. - **speckit.house-process.tips-git**: Agent tips for git and GitHub PR history: upstream tracking after branching from origin/main, scanning PR refs, confirming deleted PR description revisions, and images in PR bodies. ([alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com](https://github.com/alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/house-process/commands/speckit.house-process.tips-git.md))
- **speckit.house-process.tips-github-actions**: Agent tips for GitHub Actions: unreliable schedule triggers, billed minutes per job, read-only builds with a separate write job, bash -e in retry loops, setup-node, Playwright installs, red non-required checks, continue-on-error, jq, rulesets, npm overrides, finding runs by commit, createdAt in run listings, CodeQL PR alerts and path-injection, and proving a step ran. Read before writing or changing a workflow, a composite action, or CI checks. - **speckit.house-process.tips-owner-content**: Agent tips for content written with or about the owner: interviews and the private details people volunteer, figures from data exports, a draft reviewed once at the end, briefs for personal stories, and reading an owner's screenshot. Read before interviewing the owner, drafting a personal story or page text, or acting on an owner's review. - **speckit.house-process.tips-privacy-pipelines**: Agent tips for privacy and data pipelines: checks that report position and rule never the value, dry-running aggregate reports, versioned cache keys, shared fixtures across languages, running a new published field through the full gate, versioned raw bundles, approval hashes, scans over prose, first-match scanners, values leaking through error messages, and YAML aliases that throw. - **speckit.house-process.tips-providers**: Agent tips for external providers and file formats: intervals.icu FIT endpoints, garmin-fit-sdk, proving a response with a live call, urllib redirects and headers, credential shape checks, Decimal precision and strict Python regexes. Read before writing or changing a provider adapter or parser. ([alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com](https://github.com/alexwebber/alexanderwebber.com/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/house-process/commands/speckit.house-process.tips-providers.md))
- **speckit.house-process.tips-research**: Agent tips for research: reading a platform's terms first, product-specific API subscriptions, docs that need a real browser, verifying what a form is for before sending the owner to it, and trying a test environment's existing items before calling it broken. - **speckit.house-process.tips-secrets**: Agent tips for handling secrets: never printing a secrets file's lines, taking secrets from a file, no PowerShell pipes into gh secret set, and keeping throwaway probes to what they may print. - **speckit.house-process.tips-shell**: Agent tips for shell commands and processes: stopping servers safely, heredocs and backslashes, reading test output, Git Bash path rewriting, sed and node -e on Windows, generator reruns under autocrlf, User-Agents and re-reading settings. - **speckit.house-process.tips-testing**: Agent tips for testing, unit and end-to-end: path-filtered jobs and bot data, fakes for store interfaces, expectations taken from the data, every Playwright project locally, pointer-interception, font-dependent layout checks, timeouts, beforeAll per project, exact and scoped locators, fixture seeds, absence checks, proving checks fail, per-project counts, lazy images and screenshots, toBeInViewport on focused links, accessible names of SVGs, and failures from a sleeping machine. Read before changing page layout, CSS toggles, charts, or keyboard and screen-reader behaviour.
